   138:   const timetable=raw?.timetable||{};

  1935: function expectedSchoolPeriods(value) {
  1936:   const date = value instanceof Date ? value : neisDateToLocalDate(value);
  1937:   const weekdayNo = date?.getDay();
  1938:   if (weekdayNo === 3) return 5;

   936: async function sensitiveHash(value){
   937:   const bytes=new TextEncoder().encode(`UEP:SENSITIVE:${String(value||'')}`);
   938:   const digest=await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256',bytes);
   939:   return Array.from(new Uint8Array(digest)).map(b=>b.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('');
   940: }

  1518: function calendarEventTargetsFirstGrade(event) {
  1519:   const target = String(event?.target || "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
  1520:   const detail = String(event?.detail || "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").replace(/\(?1\s*[,·ㆍ&/]?\s*2?\s*학년부[^)]*\)?/g, " ");

  1522:   if (/교사|교직원/.test(target) && !/학생/.test(target)) return false;

  1524:   return /(?:^|[^0-9])1\s*학년/.test(text)
  1525:     || /1\s*[,·ㆍ&/]\s*2\s*학년/.test(text)

  1527:     || /전교생|전체\s*학생/.test(text);

  1672: function uepManagementRule(group='general'){

  1675: let __uepManagementFiveGroupCache=null;
  1676: function uepManagementFiveGroupKey(avg5){

  1678:   return Number.isFinite(value)?value.toFixed(2):null;
  1679: }
  1680: function uepManagementExpandedFiveGroups(){

  1683:   const cached=__uepManagementFiveGroupCache;
  1684:   if(cached&&signature.every((v,i)=>cached.signature[i]===v))return cached;

  1694: function uepBaseManagementGroup(actual9,avg5=null){
  1695:   const value=Number(actual9),fiveKey=uepManagementFiveGroupKey(avg5);
  1696:   if(!Number.isFinite(value))return 'general';
  1697:   const expanded=fiveKey?uepManagementExpandedFiveGroups():null;
  1698:   if(value<=UEP_STUDENT_MANAGEMENT_RULES.inseoul.internal||(expanded&&expanded.inseoul.has(fiveKey)))return 'inseoul';
  1699:   if(value<=UEP_STUDENT_MANAGEMENT_RULES.regional.internal||(expanded&&expanded.regional.has(fiveKey)))return 'regional';
  1700:   return 'general';
  1701: }
  1702: function uepMockCoreFor(group,mock={}){
  1703:   if(group==='inseoul')return mock.mainLevels?.length>=3&&Number(mock.mainWithin2||0)>=2&&mock.mainLevels.every(v=>v<=3);
  1704:   if(group==='regional')return Number(mock.mainWithin4||0)>=2&&Boolean(mock.allWithin5);
  1705:   return false;
  1706: }
  1707: function uepMockPotentialFor(group,mock={}){
  1708:   if(group==='inseoul')return mock.mainLevels?.length>=3&&Number(mock.mainWithin3||0)>=2&&mock.mainLevels.every(v=>v<=4);
  1709:   if(group==='regional')return mock.mainLevels?.length>=3&&Number(mock.mainWithin4||0)>=1&&mock.mainLevels.every(v=>v<=5);
  1710:   return false;
  1711: }
  1712: function uepManagementStage(internalOk,mockCore,mockPotential){
  1713:   if(internalOk&&mockCore)return 'core';
  1714:   if(internalOk&&mockPotential)return 'growth';
  1715:   if(internalOk||mockCore)return 'interest';

  1717: }
  1718: function studentCounselStatusLabel(type){

  1720: }
  1721: function studentCounselStatusClass(type){
  1722:   return type==='ok'?'ok':type==='near'?'near':'bad';
  1723: }
  1724: function studentCounselCriteria(candidate,groupKey){
  1725:   const base=candidate?.base||{}, mock=candidate?.mock||{}, prep=candidate?.prep||{};
  1726:   const actual=Number(base.actual9), hasActual=Number.isFinite(actual);
  1727:   const internalLimit=groupKey==='seoul'?uepManagementRule('inseoul').internal:groupKey==='regional'?uepManagementRule('regional').internal:null;
  1728:   let internal='bad';
  1729:   const expandedGroup=hasActual?uepBaseManagementGroup(actual,base.avg5):'general';
  1730:   const expandedOk=groupKey==='seoul'?expandedGroup==='inseoul':groupKey==='regional'?['inseoul','regional'].includes(expandedGroup):false;
  1731:   if(internalLimit==null) internal=hasActual&&actual<=4.5?'near':'bad';
  1732:   else if(expandedOk) internal='ok';
  1733:   else if(hasActual&&actual<=internalLimit+0.35) internal='near';
  1734:   const seoulMock=uepMockCoreFor('inseoul',mock);
  1735:   const regionalMock=uepMockCoreFor('regional',mock);
  1736:   let mockState=groupKey==='seoul'?(seoulMock?'ok':Number(mock.mainWithin2||0)>=1?'near':'bad'):
  1737:     groupKey==='regional'?(regionalMock?'ok':Number(mock.mainWithin4||0)>=1?'near':'bad'):
  1738:     (mock.levels?.length?(Number(mock.best||99)<=4?'near':'bad'):'bad');
  1739:   const subjectState=Number(prep.subjects||0)>0?'ok':'bad';
  1740:   const activityTotal=Number(prep.selected||0)+Number(prep.reports||0);
  1741:   const activityState=activityTotal>=2?'ok':activityTotal===1?'near':'bad';
  1742:   const reportState=Number(prep.reports||0)>=2?'ok':Number(prep.reports||0)===1?'near':'bad';
  1743:   return {internal,mock:mockState,subjects:subjectState,activity:activityState,reports:reportState,internalLimit};
  1744: }
  1745: function studentCounselDetailMarkup(candidate,groupKey){
  1746:   if(!candidate)return `<section class="counsel-side-card empty"><div class="query-empty"><b>학생을 선택해 주세요.</b><span>목록에서 학생을 선택하면 현재 위치·충족 기준·부족한 부분·앞으로 준비할 내용을 이 화면에서 바로 확인할 수 있습니다.</span></div></section>`;
  1747:   const s=candidate.student, c=studentCounselCriteria(candidate,groupKey), base=candidate.base||{}, mock=candidate.mock||{}, prep=candidate.prep||{};
  1748:   const actual=Number.isFinite(Number(base.actual9))?Number(base.actual9).toFixed(2):'-';
  1749:   const avg5=Number.isFinite(Number(base.avg5))?Number(base.avg5).toFixed(2):'-';
  1750:   const groupName=groupKey==='seoul'?'인서울 관리군':groupKey==='regional'?'지거국 관리군':'일반 관리군';
  1751:   const mockRule=groupKey==='seoul'?'국·수·영 2개≤2, 전체≤3':groupKey==='regional'?'국·수·영 2개≤4, 전체≤5':'최근 모의 성적의 상승 흐름 확인';

  1753:   const guide=[];

  1795: function readStudentCardHeatStore(){try{const raw=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(STUDENT_CARD_HEAT_STORE)||'{}');return raw&&typeof raw==='object'&&!Array.isArray(raw)?raw:{};}catch{return {};}}

  1798: function touchStudentCardHeat(student){const id=studentCardHeatId(student);if(!id)return 0;const store=readStudentCardHeatStore(),history=(Array.isArray(store[id])?store[id]:[]).map(Number).filter(Number.isFinite);history.push(Date.now());store[id]=history.filter(t=>t>=Date.now()-31*86400000).slice(-80);writeStudentCardHeatStore(store);return studentCardHeat(student);}

  2126: function dormStudentNoSort(a,b){

  2129: function dormTermRoster(mode){
  2130:   const term=dormTermDefinition(mode);if(!term)return [];
  2131:   const all=[...(readonlyCache?.dormHistory||[]),...(readonlyCache?.pastDormStudents||[]),...(readonlyCache?.dormStudents||[])];
  2132:   const seen=new Set();
  2133:   return all.filter(row=>{
  2134:     const year=String(row.year||'').replace(/\.0$/,'');
  2135:     const sem=String(row.semester||'').replace(/[^0-9]/g,'');
  2136:     const no=String(row.studentNo||'').replace(/\D/g,'');
  2137:     const inferredGrade=no?Number(no[0]):Number(String(row.className||'').match(/^([123])[- ]/)?.[1]||1);
  2138:     const grade=Number(row.grade||inferredGrade||1);
  2139:     if(year&&term.year&&year!==term.year)return false;
  2140:     if(grade!==term.grade)return false;

  2304: function dormInseoulGap(student){
  2305:   const row=admissionManagementDataset('inseoul').find(x=>String(x.student?.id||'')===String(student.id||''));
  2306:   const standard=row?.standard||{internal:uepManagementRule('inseoul').internal,mockText:uepManagementRule('inseoul').mockText};
  2307:   const actual=row?.base?.actual9;
  2308:   const internalGap=actual==null?null:Math.max(0,(actual-Number(standard.internal))/Number(standard.internal)*100);
  2309:   const levels=(row?.mock?.mainLevels||[]).filter(Number.isFinite);
  2310:   const within3=levels.filter(v=>v<=3).length,within2=levels.filter(v=>v<=2).length;
  2311:   const mockScore=levels.length?Math.min(100,((Math.min(within3,3)+Math.min(within2,2))/5)*100):null;
  2312:   const mockGap=mockScore==null?null:100-mockScore;
  2313:   const overall=[internalGap,mockGap].filter(v=>v!=null).length?Math.max(...[internalGap,mockGap].filter(v=>v!=null)):null;
  2314:   const gaps=[];
  2315:   if(actual==null)gaps.push('내신 9등급 환산자료 확인 필요');
  2316:   else if(actual>standard.internal)gaps.push(`내신 ${actual.toFixed(2)} → 핵심기준 ${Number(standard.internal).toFixed(2)} 이내 보완`);
  2317:   else gaps.push(`내신 핵심기준 충족 (${actual.toFixed(2)})`);
  2318:   if(levels.length<3)gaps.push('국·수·영 모의고사 자료 보완');
  2319:   else{
  2320:     levels.forEach((v,i)=>{if(v>3)gaps.push(`${['국어','수학','영어'][i]||'주요영역'} ${v}등급 → 3등급 이내`);});
  2321:     if(within2<2)gaps.push(`국·수·영 2등급 이내 ${within2}개 → 2개 필요`);
  2322:     if(within3===3&&within2>=2)gaps.push('모의고사 인서울 핵심기준 충족');
  2323:   }
  2324:   return {row,standard,actual,internalGap,mockGap,overall,gaps};
  2325: }

  3273: async function inputCenterRunAutoOCR(drafts){
  3274:   const targets=[];for(const d of drafts){d.ocrStatus='OCR처리중';d.ocrError='';const pages=Array.isArray(d.pages)&&d.pages.length?d.pages:[{filePath:d.filePath}];pages.forEach((p,i)=>{if(p.filePath)targets.push({key:`${d.draftId}::${i}`,path:p.filePath});});}

  3276:   const result=await window.schoolBoard?.runInputOCR?.({files:targets});if(!result?.ok){drafts.forEach(d=>{d.ocrStatus='OCR오류';d.ocrError=result?.reason||'로컬 OCR 호출 실패';});render('inputs');return;}
  3277:   const byKey=new Map((result.results||[]).map(x=>[x.key,x]));
  3278:   for(const d of drafts){const pages=Array.isArray(d.pages)&&d.pages.length?d.pages:[{filePath:d.filePath}];const texts=[],engines=new Set(),errors=[];pages.forEach((p,i)=>{const r=byKey.get(`${d.draftId}::${i}`);if(!r)return;if(r.ok&&r.text)texts.push(`[${i+1}페이지]\n${r.text}`);if(r.engine)engines.add(r.engine);if(!r.ok)errors.push(`${i+1}p ${r.reason||r.status||'OCR 실패'}`);});d.text=texts.join('\n\n').trim();d.rawText=d.text;d.ocrEngine=[...engines].join(' + ');d.ocrError=errors.join(' / ');d.ocrStatus=errors.length?(d.text?'OCR확인필요':'OCR오류'):(d.text?'OCR완료':'OCR확인필요');inputCenterApplyOcrMatch(d);}
  3279:   render('inputs');toast(`로컬 OCR 완료 · 자동매칭 ${drafts.filter(x=>x.studentNo).length}건 · 확인필요 ${drafts.filter(x=>!x.studentNo||/오류|확인필요/.test(String(x.ocrStatus||''))).length}건`);

  4590: function scorePeriodKey(row) {
  4591:   const year = String(row?.schoolYear || today.getFullYear()).replace(/[^0-9]/g, "") || String(today.getFullYear());
  4592:   let semester = String(row?.semester || "").match(/[12]/)?.[0] || "";
  4593:   if (!semester && row?.scoreType === "모의고사") {
  4594:     const month = Number(String(row.exam || "").match(/(\d{1,2})\s*월/)?.[1] || 0);
  4595:     semester = month && month >= 8 ? "2" : "1";
  4596:   }
  4597:   return `${year}-${semester || "1"}`;
  4598: }
  4599: function scorePeriodLabel(key) {
  4600:   if (!key || key === "all") return "전체 누적";
  4601:   const [year, semester] = String(key).split("-");
  4602:   return `${year}학년도 ${semester || "1"}학기`;
  4603: }
  4604: function availableScorePeriods(mode = scoreMode) {

  4607: }
  4608: function scoreExamOrder(exam) {
  4609:   const order = ["1차고사","2차고사","학기말고사","3월모의고사","6월모의고사","9월모의고사","10월모의고사","11월모의고사","수능"];
  4610:   const index = order.indexOf(exam);
  4611:   return index < 0 ? 999 : index;
  4612: }
  4613: function scoreTrend(row, allRows) {
  4614:   const orders = row.scoreType === "모의고사" ? ["3월모의고사","6월모의고사","9월모의고사","10월모의고사","11월모의고사"] : ["1차고사","2차고사","학기말고사"];
  4615:   const index = orders.indexOf(row.exam);
  4616:   if (index <= 0 || row.level === "" || row.level == null) return { text:"-", cls:"" };
  4617:   const period = scorePeriodKey(row);
  4618:   const previous = allRows.find(x => x.studentId === row.studentId && x.scoreType === row.scoreType && x.subject === row.subject && x.exam === orders[index - 1] && scorePeriodKey(x) === period);
  4619:   if (!previous || previous.level === "" || previous.level == null) return { text:"-", cls:"" };
  4620:   const diff = Number(previous.level) - Number(row.level);
  4621:   if (!Number.isFinite(diff) || diff === 0) return { text:"유지", cls:"" };
  4622:   return diff > 0 ? { text:`▲ ${Math.abs(diff)}등급`, cls:"good" } : { text:`▼ ${Math.abs(diff)}등급`, cls:"bad" };
  4623: }
  4624: function gradeToneClass(value) {
  4625:   const grade = Number(value);
  4626:   return Number.isFinite(grade) && grade >= 1 && grade <= 9 ? `grade-${grade}` : "grade-na";
  4627: }
  4628: function gradeBadgeMarkup(value, suffix = "등급") {
  4629:   if (value === "" || value === null || value === undefined || value === "-") return `<span class="grade-badge grade-na">-</span>`;
  4630:   const number = Number(value);
  4631:   if (!Number.isFinite(number)) return `<span class="grade-badge grade-na">${escapeHtml(String(value))}</span>`;
  4632:   return `<span class="grade-badge ${gradeToneClass(number)}"><b>${scoreDisplay(number,2)}</b><small>${suffix}</small></span>`;
  4633: }
  4634: function mockGradeMiniMarkup(studentId, exam) {
  4635:   const order = ["국어", "수학", "영어", "한국사"];
  4636:   const rows = (readonlyCache?.scoreRecords || []).filter(row => row.studentId === studentId && row.scoreType === "모의고사" && row.exam === exam);
  4637:   if (!rows.length) return `<span class="mock-grade-empty">자료 없음</span>`;

  4640:     if(ai>=0||bi>=0) return (ai<0?99:ai)-(bi<0?99:bi);
  4641:     return String(a.subject).localeCompare(String(b.subject),"ko");
  4642:   });
  4643:   return `<div class="mock-grade-mini-strip">${sorted.map(row=>`<span><small>${escapeHtml(row.subject)}</small><b class="${gradeToneClass(row.level)}">${escapeHtml(String(row.level || "-"))}</b></span>`).join("")}</div>`;

  4645: function scoreTableMarkup(rows, mode = scoreMode) {
  4646:   const coreOrder = mode === "mock" ? ["국어","수학","영어","한국사"] : ["공통국어1","공통수학1","공통영어1","한국사1","통합사회1","통합과학1","한문","과학탐구실험1","체육1","음악"];
  4647:   const subjects = [...new Set(rows.map(row=>row.subject).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>{
  4648:     const ai=coreOrder.indexOf(a), bi=coreOrder.indexOf(b);
  4649:     if(ai>=0||bi>=0) return (ai<0?999:ai)-(bi<0?999:bi);
  4650:     return a.localeCompare(b,"ko");
  4651:   });
  4652:   const groups = new Map();
  4653:   rows.forEach(row=>{
  4654:     const period = scorePeriodKey(row);
  4655:     const key=`${row.studentId}|${period}|${row.exam}`;
  4656:     if(!groups.has(key)) groups.set(key,{studentId:row.studentId,studentNo:row.studentNo,name:row.name,period,exam:row.exam,items:new Map()});
  4657:     groups.get(key).items.set(row.subject,row);
  4658:   });
  4659:   const grouped=[...groups.values()].sort((a,b)=>String(a.studentNo).localeCompare(String(b.studentNo)) || a.period.localeCompare(b.period) || scoreExamOrder(a.exam)-scoreExamOrder(b.exam) || String(a.exam).localeCompare(String(b.exam),"ko"));
  4660:   const cell=(row)=>{
  4661:     if(!row) return `<td class="empty-score-cell">-</td>`;
  4662:     const trend=scoreTrend(row,readonlyCache?.scoreRecords||[]);
  4663:     const trendMarkup=trend.text!=="-"?`<em class="trend ${trend.cls}">${trend.text}</em>`:"";
  4664:     if(row.scoreType==="모의고사") return `<td><div class="score-cell-primary">${gradeBadgeMarkup(row.level)}${trendMarkup}</div><small>원점수 ${scoreDisplay(row.rawScore)} · 표준 ${scoreDisplay(row.standardScore)}${row.percentile!==""?` · 백분위 ${scoreDisplay(row.percentile)}`:""}</small></td>`;
  4665:     const gradeValue = row.level === "산출제외" ? (row.achievement || "산출제외") : row.level;
  4666:     return `<td><div class="score-cell-primary">${gradeBadgeMarkup(gradeValue, Number.isFinite(Number(gradeValue))?"등급":"")}${trendMarkup}</div><small>원점수 ${scoreDisplay(row.rawScore)} · 석차 ${escapeHtml(String(row.rank||"-"))}/${escapeHtml(String(row.enrollment||"-"))}${row.achievement?` · ${escapeHtml(row.achievement)}`:""}</small></td>`;
  4667:   };
  4668:   const title = mode === "mock" ? "모의고사 과목별 등급" : "내신 과목별 등급";
  4669:   return `<div class="data-card score-matrix-card"><div class="card-head"><div><strong>${title}</strong></div><small>전체 누적 또는 선택 학기를 표시합니다.</small></div><div class="score-matrix-scroll"><table class="score-matrix-table"><thead><tr><th class="student-col">학생</th><th class="exam-col">학기·시험</th>${subjects.map(subject=>`<th>${escapeHtml(subject)}</th>`).join("")}</tr></thead><tbody>${grouped.map(group=>`<tr><th class="student-col"><b>${escapeHtml(group.studentNo)}</b><small>${escapeHtml(group.name)}</small></th><th class="exam-col"><b>${escapeHtml(scorePeriodLabel(group.period).replace("학년도 ","· "))}</b><small>${escapeHtml(group.exam)}</small></th>${subjects.map(subject=>cell(group.items.get(subject))).join("")}</tr>`).join("")}</tbody></table></div></div>`;
  4670: }

  4672: function scoreDisplay(value, digits = 1) {
  4673:   if (value === "" || value === null || value === undefined || value === "-") return "-";
  4674:   const number = Number(value);
  4675:   if (!Number.isFinite(number)) return escapeHtml(String(value));
  4676:   return Number.isInteger(number) ? String(number) : number.toFixed(digits).replace(/\.0+$/, "");
  4677: }

  4743: function runScoreQuery() {

  4788: function uepCommonCourseKey(value){
  4789:   return String(value||'').replace(/\s+/g,'').replace(/[ⅠⅡⅢ]/g,ch=>({'Ⅰ':'I','Ⅱ':'II','Ⅲ':'III'}[ch]||ch)).replace(/[*＊]/g,'');
  4790: }
  4791: function uepCommonCreditOf(row){
  4792:   const key=uepCommonCourseKey(row?.subject||row?.course||row?.courseName||row?.['과목명']);
  4793:   const curriculumRows=Array.isArray(readonlyCache?.curriculumDb)?readonlyCache.curriculumDb:[];
  4794:   const credits=[...new Set(curriculumRows.filter(c=>uepCommonCourseKey(c?.['과목명'])===key).map(c=>Number(c?.['이수단위'])).filter(n=>Number.isFinite(n)&&n>0))];
  4795:   if(credits.length===1)return credits[0];
  4796:   const direct=[row?.credit,row?.credits,row?.courseCredit,row?.unitCredit,row?.['이수학점'],row?.['이수단위'],row?.['학점']].map(Number).find(n=>Number.isFinite(n)&&n>0);
  4797:   if(direct)return direct;
  4798:   console.warn('[UEP common weighted grade] credit unresolved',{subject:row?.subject||row?.['과목명'],row});
  4799:   return null;
  4800: }
  4801: function uepCommonWeightedAverage(items,valueFn,rowFn=(x=>x)){
  4802:   const weighted=(items||[]).map(item=>{const value=Number(valueFn(item));const row=rowFn(item);const credit=uepCommonCreditOf(row);return {value,credit};}).filter(x=>Number.isFinite(x.value)&&Number.isFinite(x.credit)&&x.credit>0);
  4803:   const credits=weighted.reduce((sum,x)=>sum+x.credit,0);
  4804:   return credits?weighted.reduce((sum,x)=>sum+x.value*x.credit,0)/credits:null;
  4805: }

  4810:   const number=Number(String(value??"").replace(/[^0-9.\-]/g,""));
  4811:   return Number.isFinite(number)?number:null;
  4812: }

  4912: function internalNineGradeCut(percentile){
  4913:   if(percentile<=4)return 1;
  4914:   if(percentile<=11)return 2;
  4915:   if(percentile<=23)return 3;
  4916:   if(percentile<=40)return 4;
  4917:   if(percentile<=60)return 5;
  4918:   if(percentile<=77)return 6;
  4919:   if(percentile<=89)return 7;
  4920:   if(percentile<=96)return 8;
  4921:   return 9;
  4922: }
  4923: function internalAchievementSummary(items){
  4924:   const achievements=items
  4925:     .map(row=>({subject:String(row.subject||"").replace(/\d+$/,"").trim(),achievement:String(row.achievement||row.performance||row.level||"").trim()}))
  4926:     .filter(item=>item.subject&&item.achievement&&/^[A-E]$/i.test(item.achievement));
  4927:   if(!achievements.length)return {summary:"-",detail:""};
  4928:   const unique=[...new Map(achievements.map(item=>[`${item.subject}|${item.achievement.toUpperCase()}`,{subject:item.subject,achievement:item.achievement.toUpperCase()}])).values()];
  4929:   const detail=unique.map(item=>`${item.subject} ${item.achievement}`).join(" · ");
  4930:   return {summary:detail,detail};
  4931: }
  4932: function internalStatisticsMarkup(exam,classNo){
  4933:   const rows=scoreStatisticsRows("internal",exam,classNo);
  4934:   if(!exam)return `<div class="query-empty"><b>내신 시험을 선택하세요.</b><span>총점과 평균등급 기준 랭킹을 조회합니다.</span></div>`;
  4935:   if(!rows.length)return `<div class="query-empty"><b>${escapeHtml(exam)} 자료가 없습니다.</b><span>선택한 반 또는 성적 원본 자료를 확인하세요.</span></div>`;
  4936: 
  4937:   const groups=new Map();
  4938:   rows.forEach(row=>{
  4939:     if(!groups.has(row.studentId))groups.set(row.studentId,[]);
  4940:     groups.get(row.studentId).push(row);
  4941:   });
  4942:   const results=[...groups.entries()].map(([studentId,items])=>{
  4943:     const student=scoreStudentInfo(studentId);
  4944:     const gradeRows=items.filter(row=>{
  4945:       const level=scoreNumeric(row.level);
  4946:       return level!==null&&level>=1&&level<=5;
  4947:     });
  4948:     const scores=gradeRows.map(row=>scoreNumeric(row.rawScore)).filter(value=>value!==null);
  4949:     const levels5=gradeRows.map(row=>scoreNumeric(row.level)).filter(value=>value!==null);
  4950:     const actualNine=actualNineAverage(gradeRows);
  4951:     const levels9=actualNine.details.map(item=>item.result.grade).filter(value=>Number.isFinite(value));
  4952:     const achievement=internalAchievementSummary(items.filter(row=>{
  4953:       const level=scoreNumeric(row.level);
  4954:       return !(level!==null&&level>=1&&level<=5);
  4955:     }));
  4956:     return {
  4957:       studentId,
  4958:       student,
  4959:       total:scores.reduce((sum,value)=>sum+value,0),
  4960:       average5:uepCommonWeightedAverage(gradeRows,row=>scoreNumeric(row.level)),
  4961:       average9:uepCommonWeightedAverage(actualNine.details,item=>Number(item.result?.grade),item=>item.row||{}),
  4962:       gradeSubjects:levels5.length,
  4963:       allSubjects:gradeRows.length,
  4964:       achievement
  4965:     };
  4966:   });
  4967:   const totalRank=results.slice().sort((a,b)=>b.total-a.total||((a.average5??99)-(b.average5??99)));
  4968:   const averageRank=results.filter(item=>item.average5!==null).slice().sort((a,b)=>a.average5-b.average5||b.total-a.total);
  4969:   const totalList=totalRank.map((item,index)=>`<div class="internal-ranking-row total"><strong>${index+1}</strong>${scoreStatisticsStudentButton(item.studentId,`<b>${escapeHtml(item.student.studentNo||"")}</b><span>${escapeHtml(item.student.name||"")}</span>`)}<span>${item.allSubjects}과목</span><em>${scoreDisplay(item.total,1)}</em><small>평균등급 ${item.average5===null?"-":scoreDisplay(item.average5,2)}(5등급)</small></div>`).join("");
  4970:   const averageList=averageRank.map((item,index)=>`<div class="internal-ranking-row average"><strong>${index+1}</strong>${scoreStatisticsStudentButton(item.studentId,`<b>${escapeHtml(item.student.studentNo||"")}</b><span>${escapeHtml(item.student.name||"")}</span>`)}<span>${item.gradeSubjects}과목</span><em>${scoreDisplay(item.average5,2)}<small>(5등급)</small></em><span class="nine-grade-ref">${item.average9===null?"-":scoreDisplay(item.average9,2)}<small>(9등급 참고)</small></span><span class="achievement-ref" title="${escapeHtml(item.achievement.detail)}">${escapeHtml(item.achievement.summary)}</span><small class="total-ref">${scoreDisplay(item.total,1)}</small></div>`).join("");
  4971:   return `<div class="internal-ranking-columns"><section class="score-stat-section"><div class="score-stat-title"><div><h3>${escapeHtml(exam)} 총점 랭킹</h3><p>등급이 산출되는 과목의 원점수 합만 기준으로 정렬합니다. 성취도 A/B/C 과목은 총점에서 제외합니다.</p></div></div><div class="internal-ranking-head total"><span>순위</span><span>학생</span><span>등급산출 과목</span><span>총점</span><span>평균등급<br><small>(5등급)</small></span></div>${totalList}</section><section class="score-stat-section"><div class="score-stat-title"><div><h3>${escapeHtml(exam)} 평균등급 랭킹</h3><p>실제 5등급 평균만 랭킹에 사용합니다. 9등급 참고는 입시 등급환산과 동일하게 과목별 석차/수강자수로 계산합니다.</p></div></div><div class="internal-ranking-head average"><span>순위</span><span>학생</span><span>등급산출 과목</span><span>평균등급<br><small>(5등급)</small></span><span>평균등급<br><small>(9등급 참고)</small></span><span>성취도 과목</span><span>총점</span></div>${averageList}</section></div>`;

  5002: function fiveGradeBoundaryEnrollment() {
  5003:   const internal = (readonlyCache?.scoreRecords || []).filter(row => row.scoreType === "내신");
  5004:   const counts = new Map();
  5005:   internal.forEach(row => {
  5006:     const value = Number(String(row?.enrollment ?? "").replace(/[^0-9]/g, ""));
  5007:     if (Number.isFinite(value) && value > 0) counts.set(value, (counts.get(value) || 0) + 1);
  5008:   });
  5009:   const frequent = [...counts.entries()].sort((a,b) => b[1]-a[1] || b[0]-a[0])[0]?.[0];
  5010:   return frequent || (readonlyCache?.students || []).filter(student => !/전출|자퇴|퇴학/.test(String(student.status || "재학"))).length || 259;
  5011: }
  5012: function fiveGradeBoundaryData(enrollment) {
  5013:   const total = Math.max(1, Math.round(Number(enrollment) || 1));
  5014:   const cumulativeRates = [0.10, 0.34, 0.66, 0.90, 1];
  5015:   const cumulative = cumulativeRates.map(rate => Math.round(total * rate));
  5016:   cumulative[cumulative.length - 1] = total;
  5017:   const counts = cumulative.map((value, index) => value - (index ? cumulative[index - 1] : 0));
  5018:   return { total, cumulative, counts };
  5019: }
  5020: function fiveGradeBoundaryMarkup() {
  5021:   if (scoreMode === "mock") return "";
  5022:   const data = fiveGradeBoundaryData(fiveGradeBoundaryEnrollment());
  5023:   const grades = data.counts.map((count, index) => `<span><small>${index + 1}등급</small><b>${count}명</b><em>${index ? data.cumulative[index - 1] + 1 : 1}~${data.cumulative[index]}등</em></span>`).join("");
  5024:   return `<details class="five-grade-boundary"><summary><div><b>5등급 경계 보정</b><span>수강자 ${data.total}명 기준 · 1등급 ${data.cumulative[0]}등까지</span></div><em>기준 보기</em></summary><div class="five-grade-boundary-body"><div class="five-grade-boundary-control"><label>수강자 수<input id="fiveGradeEnrollment" type="number" min="1" max="999" value="${data.total}"></label><button class="btn secondary" id="fiveGradeApply" type="button">경계 다시 계산</button><p><b>나이스 등급을 본 정보로 사용</b><span>아래 경계표는 수강자 수에 따른 확인용 보조정보입니다. 과목별 수강자 수가 다르면 해당 인원으로 다시 계산하세요.</span></p></div><div class="five-grade-boundary-grid" id="fiveGradeBoundaryGrid">${grades}</div><small>누적 비율 10%·34%·66%·90%에 수강자 수를 곱한 뒤 반올림하여 경계를 산출합니다.</small></div></details>`;
  5025: }

  5041: 
  5042: function actualNineGrade(row) {
  5043:   let rank = Number(String(row?.rank ?? "").replace(/[^0-9.]/g,""));
  5044:   let enrollment = Number(String(row?.enrollment ?? "").replace(/[^0-9.]/g,""));
  5045:   let derived=false;
  5046:   if((!Number.isFinite(rank)||rank<=0)&&Number.isFinite(scoreNumeric(row?.rawScore))){
  5047:     const raw=scoreNumeric(row.rawScore);
  5048:     const peers=(readonlyCache?.scoreRecords||[]).filter(peer=>
  5049:       peer?.scoreType==="내신"&&String(peer.exam||"")===String(row?.exam||"")&&String(peer.subject||"")===String(row?.subject||"")&&
  5050:       String(peer.year||"")===String(row?.year||"")&&String(peer.semester||"")===String(row?.semester||"")&&Number.isFinite(scoreNumeric(peer.rawScore))
  5051:     );
  5052:     if(peers.length){
  5053:       rank=peers.filter(peer=>scoreNumeric(peer.rawScore)>raw).length+1;
  5054:       if(!Number.isFinite(enrollment)||enrollment<=0)enrollment=peers.length;
  5055:       derived=true;
  5056:     }
  5057:   }
  5058:   if (!Number.isFinite(rank) || !Number.isFinite(enrollment) || rank <= 0 || enrollment <= 0) return null;
  5059:   const percentile = rank / enrollment * 100;
  5060:   const grade = percentile <= 4 ? 1 : percentile <= 11 ? 2 : percentile <= 23 ? 3 : percentile <= 40 ? 4 : percentile <= 60 ? 5 : percentile <= 77 ? 6 : percentile <= 89 ? 7 : percentile <= 96 ? 8 : 9;
  5061:   return { grade, percentile, rank, enrollment, derived, detail:`${rank}/${enrollment}명 = 상위 ${percentile.toFixed(1)}%${derived?' · 원점수 기준 추정석차':''}` };
  5062: }
  5063: function actualNineAverage(rows) {
  5064:   const details = rows.map(row=>({row,result:actualNineGrade(row)})).filter(item=>item.result);
  5065:   const average = details.length ? details.reduce((sum,item)=>sum+item.result.grade,0)/details.length : null;
  5066:   return { average, details };
  5067: }

  5260: function latestMockSnapshot(studentId){

  5262:   const exam=names[names.length-1]||"";

  5265:   const inquiry=rows.filter(r=>!["국어","수학","영어","한국사"].includes(String(r.subject||""))).sort((a,b)=>Number(a.level||99)-Number(b.level||99)).slice(0,2);

  5267:   const inquiryLevels=inquiry.map(r=>Number(r.level)).filter(Number.isFinite);
  5268:   const levels=[...mainLevels,...inquiryLevels];
  5269:   return {exam,rows,levels,mainLevels,inquiryLevels,max:levels.length?Math.max(...levels):null,best:levels.length?Math.min(...levels):null,
  5270:     mainWithin2:mainLevels.filter(v=>v<=2).length,mainWithin3:mainLevels.filter(v=>v<=3).length,mainWithin4:mainLevels.filter(v=>v<=4).length,

  5284: function admissionManagementDataset(group){
  5285:   const rule=uepManagementRule(group);
  5286:   const standard={internal:rule.internal,name:rule.label,mockText:rule.mockText};
  5287:   const dataset=(readonlyCache?.students||[]).map(student=>{
  5288:     const base=admissionBase(student.id),mock=latestMockSnapshot(student.id),prep=studentPreparationStats(student);
  5289:     const directInternalOk=base.actual9!=null&&base.actual9<=standard.internal;
  5290:     const fiveKey=base.avg5==null?null:Number(base.avg5).toFixed(2);
  5291:     return {student,base,mock,prep,directInternalOk,fiveKey,standard};
  5292:   });
  5293:   const qualifyingFiveGroups=new Set(dataset.filter(x=>x.directInternalOk&&x.fiveKey!=null).map(x=>x.fiveKey));
  5294:   return dataset.map(x=>{
  5295:     const internalOk=x.directInternalOk||(x.fiveKey!=null&&qualifyingFiveGroups.has(x.fiveKey));
  5296:     const groupExpanded=internalOk&&!x.directInternalOk;
  5297:     const mockCore=uepMockCoreFor(group,x.mock);
  5298:     // 성장관리는 양쪽 축 모두 의미 있는 근접 상태일 때만 사용합니다. 관심관리는 한 축의 완전충족만 허용합니다.
  5299:     const mockPotential=uepMockPotentialFor(group,x.mock);
  5300:     const mockAny=mockCore||mockPotential;
  5301:     const participation=Boolean(x.prep.after||x.prep.night||x.prep.nightStudy||x.prep.selected||x.prep.reports);
  5302:     // v0.76.49 관리단계: 성적 기준만 사용합니다. 참여이력만으로 관리대상에 넣지 않습니다.
  5303:     // 핵심=내신+모의 핵심기준 모두 충족 / 성장=내신 충족+모의 일부 충족 / 관심=내신 또는 모의 한 종류만 충족 / 그 외=일반
  5304:     const stage=uepManagementStage(internalOk,mockCore,mockPotential);
  5305:     const reasons=[];
  5306:     if(x.directInternalOk)reasons.push(`9등급 평균 ${standard.internal} 이내`);
  5307:     else if(groupExpanded)reasons.push('기준 충족 학생과 동일한 5등급 평균 그룹');
  5308:     if(mockCore)reasons.push(standard.mockText);
  5309:     else if(mockPotential)reasons.push('모의고사 핵심기준 근접');
  5310:     if(participation)reasons.push('학습·프로그램 참여 이력');
  5311:     if(!reasons.length)reasons.push('현재 관리 기준 미충족');
  5312:     const managePoints=[];
  5313:     if(!mockCore)managePoints.push('취약 모의고사 영역 보완');
  5314:     if(!x.prep.after&&!x.prep.night)managePoints.push('방과후·야간심화 추천 검토');
  5315:     if(!x.prep.subjects)managePoints.push('선택과목·진로 연계 확인');
  5316:     if(!x.prep.selected)managePoints.push('계열 연계 프로그램 참여 권장');
  5317:     if(!managePoints.length)managePoints.push('상승 추이와 수능최저 준비 지속 확인');
  5318:     return {...x,internalOk,groupExpanded,mockCore,mockPotential,stage,reasons,managePoints};
  5319:   });
  5320: }

  7567: function normalizeTeacherTimetableCell(cell){
  7568:   if(!cell)return null;
  7569:   if(typeof cell==="string")return {subject:cell,room:"",changed:false};
  7570:   if(Array.isArray(cell)){
  7571:     const subject=String(cell[1]||cell[0]||"").trim();
  7572:     const room=String(cell[3]||cell[2]||"").trim();
  7573:     return subject?{subject,room,changed:false}:null;
  7574:   }
  7575:   const subject=String(cell.subject||cell.subjectName||cell.name||cell.course||cell.className||"").trim();
  7576:   const room=String(cell.location||cell.room||cell.classroom||cell.gradeClass||cell.classNo||"").trim();
  7577:   return subject?{subject,room,changed:Boolean(cell.isChanged||cell.changed||cell.changeInfo),changeInfo:cell.changeInfo||""}:null;
  7578: }

  7739: function schoolPeriodStatus(now) {
  7740:   const minute = now.getHours() * 60 + now.getMinutes();
  7741:   const periods = [[520,570],[580,630],[640,690],[700,750],[820,870],[880,930],[940,990]];
  7742:   const index = periods.findIndex(([start, end]) => minute >= start && minute <= end);
  7743:   if (index >= 0) return { index, label: `현재 ${index + 1}교시` };
  7744:   if (minute < periods[0][0]) return { index: -1, label: "일과 시작 전" };
  7745:   if (minute > periods[6][1]) return { index: -1, label: "정규수업 종료" };

  8253: function currentTeacherAccessScope(){

  8255:   const teacher=currentLoginTeacherName();

  8258:   const homeroomRow=(SOURCE_REFERENCE_DATA?.homeroom||[]).find(row=>String(row?.[2]||'').trim()===teacher);
  8259:   const grade=String(profile.grade||homeroomRow?.[0]||defaultGrade);
  8260:   const classNo=String(profile.classNo||homeroomRow?.[1]||state?.settings?.neis?.classNo||'');

  8263:   if(role==='homeroom') return {role,teacher,grade,classNo,label:classNo?`${grade}학년 전체 조회 · ${classNo}반 업무수정`:`${grade}학년 전체 조회`};
  8264:   return {role,teacher,grade,classNo,label:`${grade}학년 조회`};

  8266: function recordGradeClass(row){
  8267:   const studentNo=String(row?.studentNo||row?.number||row?.studentNumber||'').replace(/\D/g,'');
  8268:   const grade=String(row?.grade||row?.gradeNo||row?.studentGrade||(studentNo.length>=4?studentNo[0]:'')||'');
  8269:   let classNo=String(row?.classNo||row?.className||row?.class||'').replace(/[^0-9]/g,'');
  8270:   if(!classNo&&studentNo.length>=4) classNo=String(Number(studentNo.slice(1,3))||'');
  8271:   return {grade,classNo};
  8272: }
  8273: function filterRowsByCurrentTeacherScope(rows){
  8274:   const scope=currentTeacherAccessScope();
  8275:   if(!Array.isArray(rows))return [];
  8276:   if(!scope.grade&&!scope.classNo)return rows.slice();
  8277:   return rows.filter(row=>{
  8278:     const gc=recordGradeClass(row);

  8732: function dashboardStatusClassNo(row){

  8734:   if(gc.classNo)return String(Number(gc.classNo));
  8735:   const label=String(row.classLabel||row.className||'');
  8736:   const match=label.match(/(?:^|[-\s])([1-9])(?:반)?$/);
  8737:   if(match)return String(Number(match[1]));
  8738:   const studentNo=String(row.studentNo||row.studentRef?.studentNo||'').replace(/\D/g,'');
  8739:   if(/^1[1-9]\d{2}$/.test(studentNo))return studentNo.slice(1,2);
  8740:   return '';
  8741: }

  8991: function counselPriorityCandidateForStudent(student){
  8992:   const base=admissionBase(student.id),mock=latestMockSnapshot(student.id),prep=studentPreparationStats(student);
  8993:   const actual9=base.actual9;
  8994:   const reasons=[],tags=[];
  8995:   let score=0;
  8996:   if(actual9!=null){
  8997:     const seoulRule=uepManagementRule('inseoul'),regionalRule=uepManagementRule('regional');
  8998:     const d25=Math.abs(actual9-seoulRule.internal),d40=Math.abs(actual9-regionalRule.internal);
  8999:     if(d25<=seoulRule.boundary){score+=120-Math.round(d25*100);reasons.push(`9등급 평균 ${actual9.toFixed(2)} · ${seoulRule.internal.toFixed(1)} 경계`);tags.push('2.5 경계');}
  9000:     if(d40<=regionalRule.boundary){score+=105-Math.round(d40*100);reasons.push(`9등급 평균 ${actual9.toFixed(2)} · ${regionalRule.internal.toFixed(1)} 경계`);tags.push('4.0 경계');}
  9001:   }
  9002:   const managementGroup=actual9!=null?uepBaseManagementGroup(actual9,base.avg5):'general';
  9003:   const inseoulInternal=managementGroup==='inseoul';
  9004:   const inseoulMock=uepMockCoreFor('inseoul',mock);
  9005:   const regionalInternal=managementGroup==='inseoul'||managementGroup==='regional';
  9006:   const regionalMock=uepMockCoreFor('regional',mock);
  9007:   const imbalance=[];
  9008:   if(inseoulInternal&&!inseoulMock){score+=90;imbalance.push('인서울 내신 우세·모의 보완');}
  9009:   else if(!inseoulInternal&&inseoulMock){score+=92;imbalance.push('인서울 모의 우세·내신 보완');}
  9010:   if(regionalInternal&&!regionalMock){score+=72;imbalance.push('지거국 내신 우세·모의 보완');}
  9011:   else if(!regionalInternal&&regionalMock){score+=74;imbalance.push('지거국 모의 우세·내신 보완');}
  9012:   if(imbalance.length){reasons.push(...imbalance);tags.push('내신↔모의 불균형');}
  9013:   const participation=Number(prep.selected||0)+Number(prep.after||0)+Number(prep.night||0)+Number(prep.reports||0);
  9014:   if(participation===0){score+=58;reasons.push('프로그램·심화학습 참여 이력 부족');tags.push('참여 부족');}
  9015:   else if(Number(prep.selected||0)===0){score+=38;reasons.push('계열 연계 선택활동 참여 부족');tags.push('선택활동 부족');}
  9016:   if(!reasons.length)return null;
  9017:   let group='growth',stage='interest';
  9018:   if(imbalance.some(x=>x.startsWith('인서울'))||tags.includes('2.5 경계')){group='inseoul';stage='growth';}
  9019:   else if(imbalance.some(x=>x.startsWith('지거국'))||tags.includes('4.0 경계')){group='regional';stage='growth';}
  9020:   return {student,base,mock,prep,group,stage,score,reasons:[...new Set(reasons)],tags:[...new Set(tags)]};
  9021: }

  9331: function normalizeTimetableGradeClass(row){
  9332:   const directGrade=Number(row?.grade??row?.학년??0);
  9333:   const directClass=Number(row?.classNo??row?.class??row?.반??0);
  9334:   if(directGrade>=1&&directGrade<=3&&directClass>=1&&directClass<=20)return {grade:directGrade,classNo:directClass};
  9335:   const raw=String(row?.classCode??row?.classroom??row?.학급??row?.반코드??row?.['학년반']??'').trim();
  9336:   const digits=raw.replace(/\D/g,'');
  9337:   if(digits.length===3){
  9338:     const grade=Number(digits[0]),classNo=Number(digits.slice(1));
  9339:     if(grade>=1&&grade<=3&&classNo>=1&&classNo<=20)return {grade,classNo};
  9340:   }
  9341:   if(digits.length===4){
  9342:     const grade=Number(digits[0]),classNo=Number(digits.slice(1,3));
  9343:     if(grade>=1&&grade<=3&&classNo>=1&&classNo<=20)return {grade,classNo};
  9344:   }
  9345:   return {grade:directGrade||1,classNo:directClass||Number(digits)||0};
  9346: }

 10177: function studentAccessContext(student){
 10178:   const scope=currentTeacherAccessScope();
 10179:   const studentClass=String(student?.className||student?.classNo||"").replace(/[^0-9]/g,"");
 10180:   const ownClass=String(scope.classNo||"").replace(/[^0-9]/g,"");
 10181:   const role=currentWriteRoleId(); /* UEP_08283_CONTACT_EFFECTIVE_ROLE */
 10182:   const isAdmin=role==="admin";
 10183:   const isOwnHomeroom=role==="homeroom"&&ownClass&&studentClass&&String(Number(ownClass))===String(Number(studentClass));
 10184:   const isOtherHomeroom=role==="homeroom"&&!isOwnHomeroom;
 10185:   const isReadOnlyLeadership=role==="vice_principal"||role==="principal";
 10186:   const canEditContact=!isReadOnlyLeadership&&(isAdmin||role==="grade_head"||isOwnHomeroom); /* UEP_08282_CONTACT_WRITE_SCOPE */
 10187:   return {role,isAdmin,isOwnHomeroom,isOtherHomeroom,isReadOnlyLeadership,canEditContact};
 10188: }

 10193:   if(card==="score") return false;
 10194:   // 연락처·이메일·보호자 연락처는 타반 담임에게 기본 마스킹하고 필요 시 현재 화면에서만 일시 공개한다.
 10195:   return card==="contact"&&(access.isOtherHomeroom||access.isReadOnlyLeadership)&&!studentSensitiveRevealKeys.has(sensitiveRevealKey(student,card));
 10196: }

 10353: function studentUniversityBadgeEvidence(student){
 10354:   const cacheKey=`${readonlyCache?.syncedAt||''}|${student.id||student.studentNo||''}|badge-evidence-v6-discovery`;
 10355:   if(studentBadgeCache.has(cacheKey))return studentBadgeCache.get(cacheKey);
 10356:   const base=admissionBase(student.id);
 10357:   const snapshot=latestMockSnapshot(student.id);
 10358:   const mockRows=snapshot.rows||[];
 10359:   if(base.actual9==null){studentBadgeCache.set(cacheKey,[]);return [];}
 10360:   const cuts=(readonlyCache?.admissionCuts||[]).filter(cut=>String(cut.discoveryUse||'Y').toUpperCase()!=='N');
 10361:   const rules=(readonlyCache?.admissionMinimums||[]).filter(rule=>String(rule.discoveryStatus||'').trim()!=='사용보류');
 10362:   const ruleMap=new Map();
 10363:   rules.forEach(rule=>{const key=universityNameKey(rule.university);if(!key)return;if(!ruleMap.has(key))ruleMap.set(key,[]);ruleMap.get(key).push(rule);});
 10364:   const evidence=[];
 10365:   for(const cut of cuts){
 10366:     const key=universityNameKey(cut.university);if(!key)continue;
 10367:     const limit=strictAdmissionCutLimit(cut.cut);if(limit==null||base.actual9>limit)continue;
 10368:     const universityRules=ruleMap.get(key)||[];
 10369:     const compatible=universityRules.map(rule=>({rule,match:admissionPairCompatibility(cut,rule)})).filter(x=>x.match.ok).sort((a,b)=>(b.match.level||0)-(a.match.level||0));
 10370:     let accepted=null;
 10371:     for(const {rule,match} of compatible){
 10372:       const check=minimumCheck(rule,mockRows);
 10373:       if(check.result==='충족'||check.result==='미적용'){
 10374:         accepted={rule,match,check};break;
 10375:       }
 10376:     }
 10377:     if(accepted){
 10378:       const {rule,match,check}=accepted;
 10379:       evidence.push({university:String(cut.university||rule.university||''),cut,rule,limit,actual9:base.actual9,minimumResult:check.result,minimumDetail:check.detail||'',mockExam:snapshot.exam||'',mockDetail:snapshot.detail||'',pairReason:match.reason,pairLevel:match.level||0,discoveryReason:cut.discoveryReason||'',recheck:cut.recheck||'',discoveryMode:'입결+수능최저'});
 10380:       continue;
 10381:     }
 10382:     // 1학년 학생 발굴은 운호고 실제 합격 입결을 중심축으로 사용합니다.
 10383:     // 수능최저DB가 없거나 전형명이 미래 기준과 정확히 일치하지 않아도 입결 사례를 버리지 않습니다.
 10384:     // 다만 해당 대학에 수능최저 규칙이 존재하고 현재 성적으로 명확히 미충족인 경우에는 배지를 만들지 않습니다.
 10385:     const checks=universityRules.map(rule=>({rule,check:minimumCheck(rule,mockRows)}));
 10386:     const anyMet=checks.find(x=>x.check.result==='충족'||x.check.result==='미적용');
 10387:     const allDefiniteFail=checks.length>0&&checks.every(x=>x.check.result==='미충족');
 10388:     if(allDefiniteFail)continue;
 10389:     const fallback=anyMet||checks.find(x=>x.check.result==='판단보류'||x.check.result==='자료부족')||null;
 10390:     evidence.push({
 10391:       university:String(cut.university||fallback?.rule?.university||''),cut,rule:fallback?.rule||null,limit,actual9:base.actual9,
 10392:       minimumResult:fallback?.check?.result||(universityRules.length?'세부확인 필요':'수능최저 직접대응 없음'),
 10393:       minimumDetail:fallback?.check?.detail||(cut.minimumLink||'운호고 실제 합격입결 우선 발굴'),mockExam:snapshot.exam||'',mockDetail:snapshot.detail||'',
 10394:       pairReason:compatible.length?'2028 전형 큰 범주 연결':'운호고 실제 합격사례 기반 발굴',pairLevel:compatible[0]?.match?.level||1,
 10395:       discoveryReason:cut.discoveryReason||'',recheck:cut.recheck||'2029 실제 지원 시 최종 모집요강 재검증',discoveryMode:'입결우선 발굴'
 10396:     });
 10397:   }
 10398:   const best=new Map();
 10399:   evidence.forEach(item=>{const key=universityNameKey(item.university),prev=best.get(key);if(!prev||item.pairLevel>prev.pairLevel||(item.pairLevel===prev.pairLevel&&item.limit<prev.limit))best.set(key,item);});
 10400:   const result=[...best.values()].filter(item=>universityBadgeVisible(item.university)).sort((a,b)=>universityPriorityIndex(a.university)-universityPriorityIndex(b.university)||String(a.university).localeCompare(String(b.university),'ko'));
 10401:   studentBadgeCache.set(cacheKey,result);return result;
 10402: }

 10478: function studentGrowthMetric(row){
 10479:   const student=row.student, scores=uepRowsForStudent(uepStudentPerfIndex().scoreByStudent,String(student.id||''));
 10480:   const examOrder=['3월모의고사','6월모의고사','9월모의고사','10월모의고사','11월모의고사','수능'];
 10481:   const exams=examOrder.map(exam=>{const vals=scores.filter(x=>x.scoreType==='모의고사'&&x.exam===exam).map(x=>Number(x.level)).filter(Number.isFinite);return vals.length?{exam,avg:vals.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/vals.length}:null;}).filter(Boolean);
 10482:   const growth=exams.length>=2?Math.max(0,exams[0].avg-exams[exams.length-1].avg):0;
 10483:   const bundle=studentRecordBundle(student), activityTypes=new Set();
 10484:   if(row.nightAttend)activityTypes.add('야자'); if(row.afterAttend)activityTypes.add('방과후'); if(row.programCount)activityTypes.add('프로그램'); if(row.reports)activityTypes.add('보고서'); if(row.universityCount)activityTypes.add('대학배지');
 10485:   (bundle.activities||[]).forEach(a=>{const t=String(a.area||a.type||a.title||'').trim();if(t)activityTypes.add(t.slice(0,12));});
 10486:   const diversity=activityTypes.size;
 10487:   const hidden=Math.round(growth*30 + Math.min(30,row.programCount*4) + Math.min(25,row.reports*3) + Math.min(15,diversity*2));
 10488:   return {...row,growth,diversity,hidden,growthReason:exams.length>=2?`${exams[0].exam} 평균 ${exams[0].avg.toFixed(2)} → ${exams[exams.length-1].exam} ${exams[exams.length-1].avg.toFixed(2)}`:'비교 가능한 모의고사 2회 미만',diversityReason:`활동 ${diversity}개 영역 · 프로그램 ${row.programCount} · 보고서 ${row.reports}`,hiddenReason:`성적상승 ${growth.toFixed(2)} · 프로그램 ${row.programCount} · 보고서 ${row.reports} · 활동영역 ${diversity}`};
 10489: }

 11846: function openAfterschoolProgramDrawer(id) {
 11847:   const sourceProgram = [...afterSchoolProgramGroups(),...groupedAfterSchoolCourses(afterSchoolProgramGroups())].find((item) => item.id === id);
 11848:   const program = programWithOverride(sourceProgram);
 11849:   if (!program) return;
 11850:   const students = (program.students || []).slice().sort((a,b)=>Number(programStudentGrade(a))-Number(programStudentGrade(b))||Number(a.className)-Number(b.className)||Number(String(a.studentNo||"").slice(-2))-Number(String(b.studentNo||"").slice(-2)));
 11851:   const statusCounts=Object.fromEntries(AFTER_ATTENDANCE_STATUSES.map(status=>[status,0]));
 11852:   students.forEach(student=>{const status=resolvedAfterAttendanceStatus(program,student);statusCounts[status]=(statusCounts[status]||0)+1;});
 11853:   const statusSummary=AFTER_ATTENDANCE_STATUSES.map(status=>`<span class="after-status-kpi"><b>${escapeHtml(status)}</b><em>${statusCounts[status]||0}</em></span>`).join("");
 11854:   $("#drawerKicker").textContent = "AFTER-SCHOOL PROGRAM";
 11855:   $("#drawerTitle").textContent = program.title;
 11856:   $("#drawerBody").innerHTML = `<div class="program-name-pair"><div><small>실제 프로그램명</small><b>${escapeHtml(program.actualTitle||program.title)}</b></div><div><small>생활기록부 명칭</small><b class="not-record-target">생활기록부 기록 대상 아님</b></div></div><div class="program-summary afterschool-program-summary"><div><b>${program.status==="예정"?"예정 수업일":"실제 수업일"}</b><span>${escapeHtml(program.date  12159: function printProgramStudentNotice(program) {
 12160:   const students=(program.students||[]).slice().sort((a,b)=>String(a.studentNo||a.number||'').localeCompare(String(b.studentNo||b.number||'')));
 12161:   const printable=window.open('', '_blank', 'width=900,height=980');
 12162:   if(!printable)return toast('학생 안내문 인쇄 창을 열 수 없습니다.');
 12163:   const grouped={};
 12164:   students.forEach(student=>{const no=String(student.studentNo||student.number||'');const classNo=String(student.className||'').replace(/[^0-9]/g,'') || (no.length>=2?no.slice(1,2):'');const key=classNo||'기타';(grouped[key]||(grouped[key]=[])).push(student);});
 12165:   const roster=Object.keys(grouped).sort((a,b)=>Number(a||99)-Number(b||99)).map(classNo=>`<section class="class-block"><h3>${escapeHtml(classNo)}반 · ${grouped[classNo].length}명</h3><table><thead><tr><th>순번</th><th>학번</th><th>이름</th><th>확인 서명</th></tr></thead><tbody>${grouped[classNo].map((student,index)=>`<tr><td>${index+1}</td><td>${escapeHtml(student.studentNo||student.number||'')}</td><td>${escapeHtml(student.name||'이름 미확인')}</td><td class="signature"></td></tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table></section>`).join('');
 12166:   const recordLabel=program.reportRequired===false?'생활기록부·보고서 별도 기록 없음':escapeHtml(program.recordTitle||program.title||'기록 대상');

 13572:   }).filter(x=>x.science+x.social>0);

  1058:       schoolYear: text_(r[1]),

  1062:       classNo: text_(r[6]),
  1063:       studentNo: text_(r[7]),
  1064:       active: text_(r[14]),
  1065:     };
  1066:     if (student.active && student.active !== 'Y') return;
  1067:     byClassNoName.set(`${student.classNo}|${student.studentNo}|${student.name}`, student);
  1068:     byClassNo.set(`${student.classNo}|${student.studentNo}`, student);
  1069:   });
  1070: 
  1071:   return { byClassNoName, byClassNo };
  1072: }
